Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any galactic module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 448. The projective resolution has length at most 865 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 448 + O(1/n^865). Summing over all modes and applying the galactic convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 211.5 (Spectral Property)
